[EVENT: YEM78744]
Date: 2023-01-30
Country: Yemen | Location: Al Hayrah
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Air/drone strike | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of the United States (2021-2025) [External/Other forces]
Actor2: AQAP: Al Qaeda in the Arabian Peninsula [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 3
Sources: Al Arabiya; Al Mahrah Post; Khabar News Agency (Yemen); Twitter; Undisclosed Source; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 30 January 2023, a US drone targeted a car of AQAP militants with a Hellfire R9X missile as the car was standing in line to fill petrol at Al Hayrah station in As Samdah area (Marib). Its 3 occupants were killed including AQAP leader Hassan al Hadrami who is responsible for manufacturing explosive devices for the organization. Al Hadrami survived a previous drone attack on his house on 30 November.
